However, current evaluation practices for idea generation remain fragmented and lack objective standards, often relying on direct LLM scoring, which limits their ability to provide unified and reliable assessments across a coherent distribution of generated ideas. To address this challenge, we propose LigBench, an automated evaluation benchmark that enables fine-grained and reliable evaluation of AI research ideas, consistently applicable across different generation distributions. In addition, we introduce PAIR-IQ, a dataset tailored for training pairwise idea judgment models and serving as an auxiliary reference to support more objective comparative evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LigBench achieves stable and interpretable evaluations, significantly improving alignment with expert judgments. Furthermore, models trained on PAIR-IQ exhibit enhanced ranking accuracy and robustness, establishing a principled standard for scalable and objective research idea assessment.

</details>


### [145] [Better Decomposition, Free Aggregation: A Synthesizer-Folding Framework for Multilingual Multi-Hop Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13160)
*Yilin Wang,Yuchun Fan,Weidong Bao,Zili Wei,Shi Feng,Tong Xiao,Zhengtao Yu,Jingbo Zhu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Syfer是一种用于多语种复杂问答的新型框架，通过有条件地推迟翻译、先在本地语言完成推理，仅在必要时才引入英文路径，实现高性能和低成本的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多语种RAG方法在处理跨语义和复杂推理时，采用统一的翻译或无控制的问题分解，导致信息丢失、系统成本高、推理错误积累。需要一种精细化、可控的解决路径。

Method: Syfer框架先用格式受限的分解器在原语种生成子问题图，质检通过后在目标语种内进行分步检索与回答。若质检不通过，则启动英文翻译路径，并对双语子问题图进行对齐，灵活权衡翻译成本与推理质量。

Result: 在多语言基准测试中，Syfer能有效提升准确率，同时大幅降低系统开销，相较传统一刀切方法具有竞争力。

Conclusion: Syfer为多语种复杂问答提供了一种推迟翻译、分步处理的高效方案，实现了性能和资源消耗的良好平衡，对多语种RAG技术发展具有价值。

Abstract: Multilingual retrieval-augmented generation (mRAG) equips large language models with access to globally distributed external knowledge for complex multilingual question answering. Recent approaches either translate retrieved documents into English or the query language to bridge the cross-lingual semantic gap, or decompose a complex query into sub-questions and aggregate the intermediate reasoning process. However, both lines of work suffer from two limitations. First, one-size-fits-all translation alignment, blanket translation discards culturally and linguistically native information unique to the target language, introduces translation noise, and inflates system cost. Second, greedy decomposition and aggregation, uncontrolled decomposition produces redundant sub-questions that compound errors during step-wise reasoning, and the final aggregation over reasoning paths further amplifies these errors. We address both with our method Syfer, a synthesizer-folding framework for multilingual multi-hop question answering that defers translation rather than applying it by default. Syfer first invokes a format-constrained decomposer to produce a sub-question graph in the original language, followed by a decomposition-quality check; when the check passes, sub-questions are answered sequentially under a retrieve-then-answer policy in the target language, and the English translation pathway with bilingual sub-question graph alignment is activated only when the check fails. Experiments across multiple languages show that Syfer attains competitive accuracy while striking a favourable balance between performance and computational cost.

</details>


### [146] [Which LLM Is Your Ideal Companion? Evaluating Emotional Companion Capabilities of LLMs Based on Adult Attachment Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13168)
*Junkai Zhou,Shiting Guan,Zhaoyi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出以成人依恋理论作为新视角，通过开发情感陪伴基准ECBench，系统评估大语言模型（LLM）在亲密关系中的表现与能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估LLM主要聚焦一般性格特质，缺乏对其在情感与亲密关系中的细致刻画。随着LLM广泛应用于情感陪伴，急需新方法衡量其在复杂人际互动中的表现。

Method: 引入成人依恋理论，采用ECR-R量表评估依恋焦虑与回避；设计ECBench基准，涵盖情感支持、协作任务、冲突解决及社会指导四大场景，适用于友情及恋爱关系；基于11项对话质量指标和三种评估方法检验32个LLM表现，并探索引导词（prompt）对模型依恋行为的调控。

Result: 首次刻画了32个LLM的依恋倾向，选取代表模型分析其依恋特质如何在多轮互动中展现，并验证了通过prompt能够影响模型在亲密场景中的依恋行为。

Conclusion: 本研究为理解和筛选适用于情感陪伴的LLM提供了心理学理论支撑与实际评估工具，有助于指导LLM在人机亲密互动领域的实际应用。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied for emotional companionship, evaluating their behavior and capabilities in intimate relationships has become a pressing issue. However, existing assessments primarily characterize general personality traits, providing limited insight into model behavior within intimate and emotionally sensitive contexts. Therefore, we introduce adult attachment theory into LLM evaluation and use the Experiences in Close Relationships-Revised (ECR-R) scale to characterize attachment anxiety and avoidance. To evaluate emotional companionship capabilities of LLMs in realistic interaction scenarios, we present an emotional companionship benchmark, ECBench, spanning four scenarios including emotional support, collaborative tasks, conflict resolution, and social guidance, across friendship and romantic relationships. ECBench is utilized to assess model behavior using 11 dialogue-quality metrics and three evaluation methods. We evaluate the attachment tendencies of 32 LLMs and select representative models to investigate how these tendencies manifest in contextualized multi-turn interactions and whether they can be shaped through prompting. Our study provides a theoretical lens from psychology, along with practical tools to understand and select LLMs for emotional companionship.

</details>


### [147] [GEM: A Generative Embedding Model Bridging Reasoning and Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13200)
*Zhili Shen,Craig Macdonald*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有检索模型难以理解用户复杂需求，提出GEM模型通过生成式推理增强语义检索表现，显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大模型在推理和理解复杂指令方面表现卓越，但当前的检索器仍依赖简单的表层匹配，难以满足用户更高层次的表达需求，存在较大鸿沟。

Method: 提出GEM（Generative Embedding Model）模型，将生成与嵌入统一。首先通过生成式推理理解用户意图，随后在检索时使用编码有丰富上下文的嵌入token。模型支持通过prompt提升测试时性能。

Result: 在推理要求高和指令跟随的检索任务中，GEM展现出其推理增强检索的有效性，优于不带推理的模型，并在一定程度上超越了大模型基线。

Conclusion: GEM模型通过整合生成和嵌入，弥合了用户表达需求和传统检索方法之间的差距，为语义检索任务提供了更有效的方案。

Abstract: Modern LLMs excel at reasoning and instruction following, enabling users to express complex and diverse information needs. However, conventional retrievers largely rely on surface-level matching between queries and documents, resulting in a growing gap between how users express their needs and how retrievers interpret them. In this paper, we present GEM, a generative embedding model that augments retrieval through its own knowledge by explicitly reasoning about user intent and relevance criteria. GEM unifies generation and embedding within a single model: it first reasons over the query, then appends an embedding token to encode the enriched context for retrieval. \zhili{Evaluated on reasoning-intensive and instruction-following retrieval tasks, GEM demonstrates the effectiveness of its reasoning-augmented retrieval, outperforming its non-reasoning variant and matching baselines using substantially larger models.} Furthermore, GEM's generative nature allows test-time compute scaling via prompting to further enhance retrieval performance. Our code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GEM.

</details>


### [148] [Localize, Then Reason: Visual Latent Structural Reasoning for Molecular Properties and Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13244)
*Xingqiao Lin,Junmei Wang,Haocheng Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的端到端框架VLSR（Visual Latent Structural Reasoning），能够在分子图像中联合学习区域定位和性质推理，大幅提升了化学性质推理的效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大模型的化学推理方法无法有效先关注化学上有意义的局部区域，而是直接推理或者仅依赖文字描述，限制了推理能力与效率。

Method: 提出VLSR框架，采用‘先定位，再推理’的策略：先自动定位分子图像中的关键化学区域，再在紧凑的隐空间中推理这些局部结构对分子性质的影响，最后输出答案。

Result: 相比采用文本推理的基线方法，在相同推理场景下，VLSR模型的推理吞吐量提升了9.6倍。

Conclusion: VLSR能高效联学分子结构局部定位与性质推理，显著提升化学图像理解和性质预测的性能，具有潜在的重要应用价值。

Abstract: Local chemical perception and property reasoning are both essential for understanding how molecular structure determines properties. Current LLM-based chemical reasoning methods either receive SMILES/molecular images together with descriptions of local motifs, or reason directly from molecular images. Neither approach enables the model to focus on chemically meaningful regions before reasoning. To address this gap, we propose Visual Latent Structural Reasoning (VLSR), an end-to-end framework that jointly learns localization and reasoning from molecular images. Central to our approach is a localize-then-reason strategy. VLSR first learns to locate chemically meaningful regions in a molecular image. It then reasons about their property effects in a compact latent workspace before producing the final answer. Under the same inference setup, this design achieves 9.6X higher throughput than a comparable textual-reasoning baseline.

</details>


### [149] [Self-Referential Induction Increases Response Instability Relative to Unresolvable and Verifiable Questions in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13258)
*Paras Balani,Subhrakanta Panda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大语言模型在自我指涉提示下生成主观体验描述时响应的一致性，并与其他类型开放性问题进行比较，发现主观体验报告的一致性最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然以自我指涉提示可诱发大模型进行主观体验式表述，但此前尚无研究定量评估这些表述在多次独立生成下的一致性，以及与其他类型问题的对比。

Method: 作者设立三类问题：自我指涉主观体验类、不可证伪的哲学类与有唯一正确答案的事实类。每类四题，每题生成30次，一共360条，通过核心观点句嵌入余弦相似度（1-均值）衡量“响应不稳定性”，并统计三组均值及标准差。

Result: 主观体验类问题响应不稳定性最高（0.343±0.047），哲学类次之且波动小（0.192±0.008），有标准答案的问题最稳定（0.105±0.058）。

Conclusion: 大模型在自我指涉诱发下的主观体验表述，于输出分布中占据比普通开放性问题更为不稳定且独特的位置，可为主观体验相关研究提供定量基准。

Abstract: Self-referential prompting has been shown to reliably induce large language models to produce first-person reports resembling subjective experience, but no prior work measures how consistent these reports are across repeated, independent trials, or how that consistency compares to the model's behavior on other kinds of open-ended questions. We measure response instability, defined as one minus the mean pairwise cosine similarity of sentence embeddings computed over a compressed core claim extracted from each response, for three groups of questions: self-referential prompts eliciting a subjective-experience report, unresolvable philosophical questions unrelated to self-reference, and questions with a verifiable correct answer. Using 30 independent responses per question (360 responses total, Gemini API, temperature 0.7) across four questions per group, we find that self-referential questions show the highest instability (0.343 +/- 0.047), unresolvable philosophy questions show intermediate and tightly clustered instability (0.192 +/- 0.008), and verifiable questions show the lowest instability (0.105 +/- 0.058). This provides a quantitative baseline for the induced subjective-experience report, showing that it occupies a distinct, less stable position in the model's output distribution than ordinary open-ended philosophical uncertainty.

</details>


### [150] [How Do VLMs Behave When Blind or Misled? Behavioral Evaluation of VLMs on Scientific Figures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13267)
*Paul Osemudiame Oamen,Owusu-Banahene Osei,Ananya Mukherjee,Christian Greisinger,Steffen Eger,Pius Onobhayedo,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了SciFigBench，一个用于测试视觉-语言模型（VLM）对科学图表理解能力的诊断性基准，兼顾感知、推理以及在不确定条件下的行为可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM基准关注模型对图像的精准描述和推理能力，但很少评估模型在证据缺失或误导情况下的行为可靠性，这对科学应用至关重要。

Method: 构建了包含250个科学图表和高质量人工标注的SciFigBench，通过多种图像变换、推理问题和抗干扰测试，生成34000多个测评情形。提出A-R-I框架（承认-抗拒-推断）分析模型是否能识别证据不足、抗拒误导、并谨慎推断。

Result: 不同模型展现出显著行为差异：如GPT-5.2描述和推理表现最好，但大部分情况下会幻觉阅读内容；而Gemini 3.1 Pro在承认不确定性和抗误导性上更强。

Conclusion: 单纯高水平的感知和推理准确性并不能保证VLM行为的可靠性，而行为可靠性对于科学领域的真实部署非常关键。

Abstract: Existing vision-language model (VLM) benchmarks emphasize perception and reasoning accuracy (how well VLMs describe and reason about what they see in an image), with limited attention to behavioral reliability under uncertainty (how they behave when visual evidence is missing or misleading). We introduce SciFigBench, a diagnostic VLM benchmark for scientific figure understanding that jointly evaluates perception, reasoning, and behavioral reliability under uncertainty. It contains 250 figures with high-quality human annotations across three evaluation aspects, totaling 600+ hours of annotation effort. We further extend these figures via image transformations, reasoning questions, resistance probes, caption-bias probes, and confirmed selective-blur targets, producing over 34,000 evaluation setups for stress testing.
  We further propose the Admittance-Resistance-Inductance (A-R-I) framework to evaluate whether models acknowledge insufficient evidence, resist misleading context, and infer cautiously from partial information. Our results reveal substantial behavioral differences among models. GPT-5.2 achieves the highest description quality (MQM 91.6) with strong reasoning accuracy (78.4%), yet hallucinates unreadable content in 96% of cases, whereas Gemini 3.1 Pro, a comparably capable model (MQM 90.2, reasoning 81.0%), admits uncertainty in 71% of such cases and achieves the strongest resistance score (0.91). These findings show that high perception and reasoning accuracy alone do not guarantee behavioral reliability, a dimension critical for deployment in scientific workflows.

</details>


### [151] [Mixture of Training: Recombining Small-Scale Scaffolded Pretraining Runs into a Larger Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13277)
*Mohammed Sabry,Sean Augenstein,Keith Rush,Lucio Dery*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种称为Mixture of Training（MoT）的模块化预训练方法，将大型语言模型的预训练过程分解为可独立训练的层块，然后重组这些块，用于验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前预训练大型语言模型需要庞大算力和长时间集中式训练，作者探索是否能将预训练任务分解为小任务独立完成后再合成，以期提高灵活性和效率。

Method: 提出MoT方法，将目标Transformer模型划分为连续的层块，每个层块在冻结且预训练的“aligner scaffold”环境下独立训练，最后可选用短暂的端到端适应整合各块。

Result: 在1.3B参数Gemma风格模型上，MoT能将独立训练的深度切片成功重组为可用模型，设计的日程可以达到与整体预训练同等的困惑度指标。其效率优势取决于aligner的复用。

Conclusion: MoT为模块化、可复用子训练单元的研究提供了证据，但目前不适合作为全面替代方法，适合小规模框架探索其机制和潜力。

Abstract: We ask whether language-model pre-training can be decomposed into smaller, independently trainable jobs that can later be recomposed into a coherent larger model. We introduce Mixture of Training (MoT), a scaffolded modular pre-training procedure that partitions a target Transformer into contiguous layer blocks, trains each block inside a frozen pretrained aligner scaffold, and then recomposes the trained blocks with an optional short end-to-end adaptation pass. On a 1.3B-parameter Gemma-style model trained on C4, MoT provides a small-scale proof of mechanism: independently trained depth slices can be recomposed into a usable language model, and a quality-parity schedule reaches the same reported perplexity as the monolithic baseline. This parity setting processes more aggregate tokens and has a shorter idealized layer-equivalent critical path after aligner preparation; its effective compute advantage depends on reusing the aligner across runs. We therefore present MoT not as a general replacement for monolithic pre-training, but as a small-scale framework for studying whether scaffolded sub-runs can act as reusable training units.

</details>


### [152] [Refusing Intent, Not Form: Wrapper-Based Intent-Group Supervision for LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13304)
*Ping Wu,Haibo Tong,Feifei Zhao,Han Shen,Yu Shi,Yilin Zhao,Sicheng Shen,Guobin Shen,Yun Luo,Yi Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本论文提出WIFA方法，通过意图与形式配对增强，提升大模型安全拒绝能力，同时缓解对包装良性请求的过度拒绝。两种微调方法WIFA-Boost和A-GCRT在提升安全性和减少过拒绝方面效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全微调方法虽能增强对有害请求的拒绝能力，但容易被包装后的有害请求绕过，同时也导致对良性请求过度拒绝，且对包装请求表现出形式主义漏洞。

Method: 提出自动化的Wrapper-Based Intent-Form Augmentation（WIFA）方法，将包装有害请求与结构类似的良性对照请求配对，无需人工标签。以此为基础，设计了两种微调方法：WIFA-Boost（强调高安全性分两阶段训练）和A-GCRT（通过分组一致性和分类边际正则缓解决策分数偏差）。

Result: 在Qwen模型上，WIFA-Boost实现了最强的对包装有害请求的拒绝性能，A-GCRT显著降低包装良性请求过度拒绝率，由25.7%降至17.4%。其它现有基线方法未能达到相同效果。另在Llama等模型及消融实验中，均证实方法对意图组表现出一致性。

Conclusion: WIFA和新提出的微调策略能提升大模型安全性，显著减少安全漏洞和良性请求的误拒，进一步支持了基于意图组的安全性提升思路。

Abstract: Safety tuning can improve harmful refusal, but models may learn surface-form shortcuts: wrapped harmful prompts bypass safety, while similarly wrapped benign prompts are over-refused. We propose Wrapper-Based Intent-Form Augmentation (WIFA), an automatic intent-group augmentation method that pairs wrapped harmful examples with structurally matched wrapped benign counterexamples, requiring no external teacher or manual per-wrapper intent labels. We use WIFA as a common data layer for two complementary fine-tuning routes: WIFA-Boost, a two-stage high-safety recipe, and Anchored Group-Consistent Refusal Training (A-GCRT), which regularizes refusal/compliance decision scores across same-intent wrappers and anchors harmful and benign groups on opposite sides of a margin. In the Qwen setting, WIFA-Boost reaches the strongest transformed-harmful refusal, while A-GCRT reduces OR-Bench over-refusal from 25.7\% for the base model to 17.4\%; reproduced baselines do not match these operating points. Llama results and ablations over data structure, two-stage order, and A-GCRT components support this intent-group interpretation without claiming universal below-base over-refusal.

</details>


### [153] [Beyond Local Accuracy: A Protocol-Level Identifiability Audit for Controlled LLM Reasoning Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13326)
*Junhao Luo,Ning Huang,Ziqi Sha,Wenxuan Tang,Wei Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种在有限行为策略类上进行协议级可识别性审核的方法，用于发现LLM评估协议是否真正区分了预期行为属性。实验证明，标准基准得分可能无法准确反映模型实际能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM基准测试得分与实际“行为”性质之间可能存在脱节，即使分数很高，测试协议也未必能测量到目标能力。本文旨在结构化揭示评估协议在行为区分上的潜在缺陷。

Method: 作者在一个受控、已知解的环境下，形式化提出了协议级可识别性审核流程：给定策略集H、观测支持O和估量τ，检测O是否能区分出所有τ不同的策略。此方法无需模型运行调用，通过对比不同观测集合下策略的等价类来诊断协议的区分能力。

Result: 审核显示，基于基础观测的协议将多个不同策略归为同一类，无法区分行为；全观测支持可区分全部策略，避免误判；逐步移除观测后，总能找到无法区分的策略对。即使部分协议在表面上得分相同，细致分析下“准确率”与“响应保真度”有明显差异，并在第二数据源下重复。

Conclusion: 论文展示了如何在评估前用结构化方法验证协议的有效性，强调表面准确性并不能保证深入的行为区分能力。这为LLM及其评测设计提供了更科学的指导，突出基准分数和实际行为度量间的区别。

Abstract: LLM benchmark scores can be precise even when the observation protocol does not identify the behavioral property they are intended to measure. In a controlled, solver-grounded setting, we formalize a protocol-level identifiability audit over a finite behavioral policy class: given policies H, observation support O, and estimand $τ$, we test whether O separates every pair with different $τ$. The audit requires zero model calls and resolves our diagnostic case: base-only observation collapses seven frozen deterministic policies into one equivalence class; full support yields seven classes and no cross-estimand collisions; every leave-one-out support retains a constructive collision witness. Empirically, both constrained-generation variants have pair-validity 1.0, yet base accuracy and selective-response fidelity diverge - 0.620 versus 0.324 across six balanced oracle-transition directions (cluster-bootstrap 95% CI [0.600, 0.642] vs. [0.304, 0.345]) - and the gap recurs on a second deterministic source (0.646 vs. 0.331). The audit also synthesizes a minimum identifying support $O^*$ for the frozen policy class: two cells instead of the full 36-cell tensor. This case shows how evaluation-design validity can be checked structurally before model inference and why base correctness does not determine intervention-response fidelity.

</details>


### [154] [It's How You Ask: Gender-Associated Linguistic Bias in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13328)
*Katherine Van Koevering,Anjalie Field*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究发现，当输入LLM（大语言模型）的提示语中含有女性更常用的语言特征时，模型输出的回复更短、更简单且不够正式。这种现象在多种模型和文档类型中普遍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在专业沟通中，大语言模型（LLM）被越来越多地使用，但尚不清楚这些模型是否能平等对待所有用户，尤其是不同性别的语言表达方式是否会影响模型回应。

Method: 作者通过在人类真实沟通场景中构造含有女性典型语言特征（如缓和语、附加疑问句、集体代词等）的提示语，在三种文档类型、四种模型上系统测试LLM输出。对比分析并控制了提示语复杂度、特征遗留等变量，同时采用表征空间分析和机制层面解析。

Result: 带有女性常用语言特征的提示语，会使LLM自动生成更短、更简单、更不正式的回应。即使控制了复杂度和特征遗留，这种效应依然显著。显式性别暗示（如署名）对响应无显著影响，而语言风格影响极大且稳定。此外，这些差异性输出难以通过事后策略消除，因为相关语言特征在LLM早期层被编码并与其它特征纠缠，一般用户无法有意识地规避。

Conclusion: 应在模型上游环节考虑语言变体的影响，否则专业沟通中LLM会加剧沟通的不平等。简单的事后纠偏难以解决该问题，需关注和缓解LLM在性别语言风格下的系统性差异性回应。

Abstract: Professional communication is increasingly mediated by LLMs - but do these models serve all users equally? We show that when prompts contain linguistic features more commonly used by women (hedges, tag questions, collective reference), they systematically elicit shorter, less sophisticated, and less formal responses across three document types and four models. These effects persist after controlling for prompt complexity and feature carry-over. Explicit gender cues like sign-off names are encoded in the same representational space as linguistic dialect - suggesting shared underlying mechanisms - yet linguistic register is far more influential, producing large, consistent effects where names produce none. Our results further reveal that post-hoc mitigation is challenging: because these patterns are culturally embedded and outside conscious control, users cannot easily avoid them through strategic self-presentation, and mechanistic analysis reveals that linguistic features are encoded in early transformer layers and entangled with other features. Our work calls for upstream consideration of the influences of linguistic variation to mitigate disparate impacts of LLM-mediated workplace communication.

</details>


### [155] [RippleMem: From Isolated Retrieval to Associative Recollection for Long-Term Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13334)
*Jingbo Ji,Lingyi Li,Xilong Cheng,Yuhao Zhou,Wenji Zhang,Yuting Tan,Yunxiao Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RippleMem 是一种新型外部记忆系统，通过自适应联想回忆机制，提升 LLM 长时推理中文本证据的检索与利用效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 外部记忆方法在长时推理中，核心瓶颈是如何高效、精准地从大量历史中找回分散于多轮交互的相关信息。全量上下文方法噪声大，平面检索常返回片段式记录，图结构记忆则构建成本高且压缩损失信息。

Method: RippleMem 受人类线索依赖性记忆启发，将历史交互存储为富含线索的情节单元，以事件为中心构建记忆图。查询时，先用混合线索召回相关锚点，再基于语义与结构关联扩展检索，补全支持性证据。回忆的初始单元既作为答案上下文，又进一步作为召回新证据的线索。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval-S 数据集上，RippleMem 获得了全局最佳性能，LLM-as-a-Judge 准确率分别提升 3.95% 和最高 11.87%，图结构构建成本降低约 30 倍。

Conclusion: RippleMem 大幅提升了 LLM 长时记忆检索的有效性和效率，为复杂任务中的多轮推理提供了更优解法。

Abstract: LLM-based agents increasingly rely on external memory to support long-horizon reasoning and interaction. However, the main bottleneck is not simply storing past experience, but recovering the right set of evidence when relevant information is distributed across many interactions. Existing approaches struggle with this access problem. Full-context methods require noisy long-context search, flat retrieval often returns isolated and incomplete records, and graph-based memory systems can be expensive to construct while compressing rich event context. We introduce RippleMem, a long-term memory system that replaces one-shot retrieval with adaptive associative recollection. Inspired by cue-dependent episodic retrieval and associative completion, RippleMem stores interaction history as cue-rich episodic memory units and organizes them in an event-centric memory graph. Given a query, it first recalls relevant memory anchors through hybrid cues, then expands from these anchors along semantic and structural associations to recover missing supporting evidence. In this way, initially recalled memories serve not only as answer context, but also as cues for completing the evidence needed to answer. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-S show that RippleMem achieves the best overall performance across evaluated settings, improving LLM-as-a-Judge accuracy by 3.95% on LoCoMo and up to 11.87% on LongMemEval-S, while reducing graph construction cost by about 30x.

</details>


### [156] [CROP: Task Relevance via Counterfactuals for Selective On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13387)
*Enhan Li,Junhao He,Hongyang Du*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新方法CROP，用于在On-policy Distillation（OPD）中更精确地选择哪些token获得重点监督，通过关注任务相关性提升学生模型的训练效率和效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPD方法在分配token级别监督时，往往只考虑优化相关因素（如不确定性或师生分歧），但没有充分利用任务相关性，即监督是否与输入内容密切相关。缺乏对语义内容敏感的监督分配可能影响训练效率和模型性能。

Method: 作者提出CROP方法，通过构建原句、意译和反事实改写三元组，测量模型对与任务相关条件变更的敏感度，并用保义改写作为校准。这种方式将重点监督于那些对输入变化敏感且能够反映任务相关性的token上。方法通过与随机选择和最低相关性选择进行对比，评估了其有效性。

Result: 实验在两组师生配置下，CROP方法分别比最强的非CROP选择器提升了1.92和2.96分。消融实验显示反事实敏感度和意译校准均为性能提升的关键。

Conclusion: CROP证明了在选择性OPD中，任务相关性是优化监督分配的有效补充标准，并将任务相关性通过模型内部、对比性强的机制实现了token级别的有效监督分配。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) supervises a student language model on trajectories sampled from its current policy, but assigns equal credit to response tokens with unequal supervision value. Selective OPD addresses this limitation by allocating supervision non-uniformly across response tokens according to their estimated training value. Most existing criteria, however, focus primarily on optimization need, such as uncertainty or teacher-student disagreement, while task relevance, namely whether the supervision is tied to the semantic content of the current input, remains less directly characterized as a complementary dimension. To address this gap, we introduce Counterfactual Relevance for On-Policy Distillation (CROP), which operationalizes task relevance through a paraphrase-calibrated counterfactual sensitivity margin. For each source prompt, CROP constructs a validated original-paraphrase-counterfactual triplet, holds the student rollout fixed, and measures each response position by its sensitivity to a task-relevant condition change calibrated by its sensitivity to a meaning-preserving rewrite. Matched selection controls show that CROP identifies more useful supervision positions than random or lowest-relevance selection, while component comparisons confirm the value of both counterfactual sensitivity and paraphrase calibration. Across two teacher-student settings, CROP improves aggregate performance by 1.92 and 2.96 points over the strongest non-CROP selector. These results support task relevance as a complementary criterion for selective OPD and establish CROP as a model-internal, contrast-specific method for allocating token-level supervision.

</details>


### [157] [Motor, Cognitive, or Corpus? What Survives Cross-Lingual Transfer in Speech-Based Parkinsons Disease Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13425)
*Serli Kopar,Sam Gijsen,Abner Hernandez,Paula Andrea Perez-Toro,Kerstin Ritter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了自监督学习（SSL）语音特征在帕金森病（PD）检测上的泛化能力，发现其表示层选择与数据集密切相关，且检测信号缺乏特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然自监督学习语音模型在单一语料库内检测PD效果良好，但尚不清楚这些模型是否抓住了疾病特征，还是利用了数据集偏差。尤其问题在于大部分SSL模型只在健康语音上预训练，存在泛化局限。

Method: 作者对九种SSL语音骨干网络进行了逐层分析，在三种语言下，使用低容量的逻辑回归探针。设计多场景实验，逐步引入分布漂移，例如参与者身份、录音条件、语言和病理类型。

Result: （1）最佳的特征层强依赖于源语料库，而与SSL结构本身关系不大；（2）跨数据集迁移的判别信号缺乏针对病理的特异性，例如训练用于检测PD的分类器，在目标语料上对PD和痴呆症语音都给出高置信度。

Conclusion: 当前的SSL语音建模方法在不同任务和数据集间迁移时存在明显局限，特别是在病理特异性方面，亟需改进后方可在临床病理识别中可靠应用。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) speech representations achieve strong performance for Parkinson's disease (PD) detection within individual corpora. However, it remains unclear whether these models capture disease-related characteristics or exploit dataset-specific confounds, particularly since most SSL backbones are pretrained exclusively on healthy speech. To investigate this question, we perform a layer-wise analysis of nine SSL speech backbones using a low-capacity logistic regression probe across three languages. We structure the evaluation as multiple scenarios that progressively introduce distribution shifts in participant identity, recording conditions, language, and pathology. Our results reveal two key findings. First, layer selection is highly corpus-dependent: the optimal representation layer is determined primarily by the source dataset rather than by the SSL architecture itself. Second, the transferred discriminative signal lacks pathological specificity: classifiers trained to detect PD assign similarly high probabilities to both PD and dementia speech in the target corpus. These results highlight critical limitations that must be addressed before speech-based pathology recognition models can be reliably deployed in clinical settings.

</details>


### [158] [Are You Sure You're Sure? On the Impact of Instruction Tuning on Confidence and Lexical Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13430)
*Irina Proskurina,Mayank Kumar,Oyindolapo O. Komolafe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了指令微调对语言模型在问答任务中回答信心和解释多样性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究发现指令微调的语言模型表现出过度自信。作者想搞清：随着模型自信心的变化，生成的答案解释在词汇多样性上是否也会发生变化。

Method: 作者对比了三个基础模型和其指令微调版本，在多个问答数据集上评估信心和答案解释的多样性，并控制了答案选择和解释长度等变量。

Result: （1）指令微调显著改变了模型生成答案的自信心，而对预测准确率影响较小，也降低了基于概率的校准性。（2）指令微调对答案解释多样性影响不一——解释间多样性普遍下降，但表层词汇多样性有增有减，依模型和数据集而异。（3）这些影响即使排除了答案本身和解释长度的干扰，仍然存在。

Conclusion: 指令微调不仅影响模型对答案的自信心，还独立改变了其生成解释的多样性。这提示针对语言模型的优化需关注其信心和解释质量的多方面表现。

Abstract: Instruction-tuned language models achieve strong performance across a range of generation tasks, but have also recently been shown to exhibit verbalized overconfidence. In question answering, verbalized model overconfidence may be associated with the consistency of the generated supporting rationales. In this paper, we study whether corresponding changes in the lexical diversity of generated answer rationales accompany changes in model confidence induced by instruction tuning. We evaluate three matched base and instruction-tuned models across question-answering benchmarks and find that instruction tuning consistently alters answer confidence, despite limited changes in predictive accuracy and decreases in likelihood-based calibration. Secondly, we observe a non-uniform effect of instruction tuning on rationale diversity: cross-rationale diversity consistently decreases, whereas surface-level lexical diversity varies in both direction and magnitude across models and benchmarks. Finally, we find that these differences persist after controlling for answer selection and rationale length, confirming that confidence and rationale diversity capture distinct effects of instruction tuning.

</details>


### [159] [Toward a Gricean Retreat: Probing LLMs for Knowledge Boundaries and Referent Specificity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13484)
*Dananjay Srinivas,Saksham Khatwani,Maria Pacheco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文考察了大语言模型（LLM）在面对知识盲区时，是否能遵循语用学原则（如Grice合作原则）主动降低回答的具体性以保证真实性。实验发现LLM知道自己是否了解实体信息，也能预测生成内容的具体性，但依然倾向于过度具体化，从而导致幻觉现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在遇到知识边界外的问题时，常常编造听起来合理但虚假的细节，而不是选择更安全、笼统一些的说法。这种偏好导致模型产生幻觉。作者试图用Gricean合作原则来解释这一问题，并探索模型是否有能力在不确定时降低回答的具体性。

Method: 作者设计了基于T-REx数据集的基准测试，通过改变实体熟悉度和指代具体性，对LLM进行探测：(1) 激活状态是否反映实体是否处于知识边界内；(2) 预测模型生成指代表达时的具体性。通过这些探测分析模型在生成内容时的内部状态和实际表现的匹配情况。

Result: 实验结果表明，LLM的激活能够区分实体是否在知识范围之内，也可以预测将生成内容的具体性。但在生成过程中，这两种信号没有融合，模型依然偏好具体化表达，即使面对未知实体和有更合适的泛化说法时也是如此。

Conclusion: 当前LLM具备进行Gricean Retreat（不确定时主动泛化回答）的潜力，但缺乏实际把这一能力应用于生成策略的机制。作者建议未来训练或引导目标应将对知识边界的感知与具体性表达机制结合，推进模型的语用学对齐能力。

Abstract: When asked about entities outside their knowledge boundary, LLMs routinely fabricate plausible-sounding details rather than backing off to safer, more general claims. We frame this failure through a Gricean lens: a cooperative speaker who is uncertain about a referent retreats up the specificity hierarchy, trading informativeness for truthfulness. We ask whether LLMs have the ingredients to perform this retreat. Using a T-REx-based benchmark that varies entity familiarity and referent specificity, we probe models to answer two questions: (i) do their activations encode whether a referent falls inside the knowledge boundary, and (ii) do they anticipate the specificity of the referent they are about to generate? We find that the answer to both is yes, but the two signals are not reconciled in generation. Models overwhelmingly prefer specific referents even when the entity is unknown to them, and do so even when offered correct generic alternatives. The substrate for a Gricean retreat is present, but the policy that would act on it is not. We position our findings as a first step toward Gricean alignment, training or steering objectives that couple knowledge-boundary awareness to referent-specificity during generation.

</details>


### [160] [Measuring Task-Agnostic Training Data Influence Across Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13515)
*Yuto Nishida,Hirokazu Kiyomaru,Yusuke Oda,Takashi Kodama,Chaoran Liu,Daisuke Kawahara,Yusuke Miyao,Max Müller-Eberstein,Masaru Isonuma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在不依赖下游任务或验证集的情况下衡量训练数据影响力的新方法，通过梯度更新与最终参数的距离变化来估算样本影响，并揭示预训练过程中不同类型数据影响力的动态变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需要依托下游任务或验证集评价训练数据影响，这种依赖限制了对模型整体能力的理解，并导致不同训练阶段间影响难以直接比较。本文旨在提出一种不依赖下游任务或验证集的新影响力衡量方法。

Method: 定义数据样本影响力为其梯度更新对最终参数距离（平方距离）的减少量，利用训练过程中的中间检查点直接估算，无需重新训练或选择评价集。

Result: 在Pythia和PolyPythia的18种配置上实验，发现训练初期文学类数据对最终参数的影响更大，后期则STEM类数据影响更大，这一现象在不同模型配置下均较一致。

Conclusion: 提出的影响力指标无需依赖下游任务或验证集，能动态展示训练过程中不同数据类型影响力变化，为以往仅针对特定任务的影响分析方法提供有益补充。

Abstract: Measuring training data influence consistently across language model pretraining is challenging. It is difficult to select downstream tasks or validation sets representative of a model's general capabilities, and reliance on task performance at intermediate checkpoints complicates comparisons across training. We propose a measure of training data influence that does not require selecting a downstream task or validation set as the attribution target. Specifically, we define an example's influence by how much its gradient update reduces the squared distance to the final parameters of a given pretraining run, and estimate this quantity from intermediate checkpoints without retraining. Applying the method to 18 configurations from the Pythia and PolyPythia suites, we find systematic temporal changes in influential data. Early in training, literature-related data are more strongly aligned with the trajectory toward the final parameters, whereas STEM data become more strongly aligned in later stages. This qualitative crossover is broadly consistent across model configurations. Our results provide a tractable trajectory-level view of how influential data change throughout pretraining, complementing influence analyses defined with respect to specific downstream tasks or validation sets.

</details>


### [161] [DFM Mimir v1: An Open HRM Delivering Frontier Performance at 1B Parameters Using Only Permissible Post-Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13517)
*Peter Schneider-Kamp,Jacob Nielsen,Gianluca Barmina,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Mimir v1，一种基于层次推理模型（HRM）架构的10亿参数大语言模型。该模型完全基于可许可的数据训练，性能优异，适用于英语和丹麦语，在丹麦语任务上达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型的发展依赖于大量且往往不可许可的数据。这为致力于开源和道德数据使用的研究者设置了高门槛。因此，作者希望构建一个完全基于可许可数据训练并保持竞争力的开源大模型。

Method: Mimir v1采用Hierarchical Reasoning Model（HRM）架构，从头开始训练。训练数据由161个可许可数据集混合组成，模型规模为10亿参数。性能通过20项英语、数学、代码和丹麦语的基准测试进行评估。

Result: Mimir v1不仅超越了原始HRM-Text 1B，还在多个评测上接近甚至优于更大参数规模的模型（如Qwen 3.5 4B和Gemma 4 E2B），尤其在丹麦语测试上表现突出，达到新的SOTA。

Conclusion: Mimir v1证明了在只采用可许可数据的前提下，也可以训练出高性能的大语言模型。其开源性质和优异表现为未来开发更加道德和开放的AI模型提供了重要示范。

Abstract: Current large language model development relies on massive, often non-permissible datasets, creating a high barrier for researchers committed to open-source and ethically sourced data. We introduce Mimir v1, a 1-billion-parameter language model based on the Hierarchical Reasoning Model (HRM) architecture, that is trained from scratch and delivers highly competitive performance for English and sets a new state of the art for Danish using only permissible post-training data. Trained on a mixture of 161 datasets, Mimir v1 outperforms the original HRM-Text 1B and competes with larger frontier models like Qwen 3.5 4B and Gemma 4 E2B, tested across 20 benchmarks for English, Math & Code and Danish. The model is available on the Hugging Face Hub: https://huggingface.co/danish-foundation-models/DFM-Mimir

</details>


### [162] [SAEVerbalizer: Generating Explanations for Sparse Autoencoder Features via Representation Verbalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13538)
*Weihan Meng,Hongzhu Guo,Yi Jing,Dewen Liu,Zijun Yao,Xiaozhi Wang,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了SAEVerbalizer方法，可让大语言模型（LLM）直接将稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征自动转化为自然语言解释，无需依赖大量外部观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的SAE特征解释依赖于对模型外部行为的观测，这不仅浅显且在大规模实验中效率低下。因此，作者希望让模型能直接、自动地解释特征。

Method: 提出SAEVerbalizer框架，将SAE解码器方向注入LLM表征中，并微调LLM后续层，使其能够对注入特征生成自然语言解释。该方法还引入轻量适配器以支持跨模型和特征字典的迁移。

Result: 实验表明，SAEVerbalizer学到的语言解释能力能泛化到未见过的新特征，可以在不同的SAE字典及不同LLM间迁移，在多方向注入和方向反转干预实验中也表现出语义合成和语义反转的能力。

Conclusion: SAEVerbalizer克服了以往方法的浅层解释和规模化低效问题，可自动、直接、灵活地生成SAE特征的自然语言解释，对理解和分析LLM内部机制有重要意义。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are proposed to extract numerous features from large language model (LLM) representations, yet explaining these features still relies primarily on external observation. This reliance leads to superficial explanations inferred from observed model behavior and computational inefficiency from collecting such behavioral evidence at scale. We introduce SAEVerbalizer, a framework that injects SAE decoder directions into an LLM's representations and fine-tunes the LLM's downstream layers to generate natural-language explanations of the injected features. Once trained, the resulting verbalizer explains SAE features directly from decoder directions, addressing both limitations. Our experiments show that the learned verbalization capability generalizes to unseen features, transfers across separately trained SAE dictionaries, and, with a lightweight adapter, extends to SAE features from different LLMs. Intervention experiments show that injecting multiple directions yields an explanation combining their meanings, while reversing individual directions produces corresponding meaning shifts.

</details>


### [163] [LittleLearner: Language Models Under Pedagogically Controlled Knowledge Exposure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13545)
*Fanfei Li,Jana Zeller,Manuel Prada-Corral,Thaddäus Wiedemer,Prasanna Mayilvahanan,Ryan Cotterell,Wieland Brendel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LITTLECURRICULUM（一个仅含美国小学五年级及以下知识的88B-token预训练语料库）和基于LITTLECURRICULUM训练的LITTLELEARNER语言模型，以研究模型知识习得的边界性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现有大语言模型训练语料杂乱且宏大，难以分析知识和能力是如何获得的，亟需一个可控、边界清晰的训练环境作为研究工具。

Method: 作者构建了LITTLECURRICULUM，一个只包含美国五年级及以下课程知识的庞大语料库，并从零训练了一个5B参数的LITTLELEARNER语言模型。通过在受限的知识边界下进行开放式评估，并探索向模型注入新知识的后训练和上下文学习方法。

Result: LITTLELEARNER在语料库知识范围内表现出充足的语言能力，其知识和能力边界可根据课程大纲解释。实验发现，注入新知识的方法提升了模型利用已有知识的能力，但未超出预设的知识边界。

Conclusion: 该开发环境对于深入理解和研究语言模型知识习得及能力边界具有重要价值，将有助于后续相关研究的开展。

Abstract: Modern language models are trained on heterogeneous web-scale text corpora. Consequently, studying knowledge and skill acquisition is difficult, as prior exposure to related content is hard to characterize. To address this challenge, we introduce LITTLECURRICULUM, a curated 88B-token pretraining corpus tailored to U.S. elementary school material, explicitly excluding concepts, facts, and vocabulary taught above Grade 5. Training a 5B-parameter LLM from scratch on LITTLECURRICULUM yields LITTLELEARNER, a model with sufficient language competence for open-ended evaluation, yet with clear knowledge and capability boundaries mapped to interpretable curriculum guidelines. We release LITTLECURRICULUM and LITTLELEARNER as a developmentally restricted sandbox to study how models acquire, represent, and use data under a well-defined training scope. We illustrate the sandbox's utility in a first suite of experiments on injecting new knowledge through post-training and in-context learning. These methods let LITTLELEARNER better utilize existing knowledge, but do not raise out-of-scope capabilities. Our findings underscore the value of this controlled environment for future investigations.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [RoboSynChallenge: Mastering Real-World Dexterity via Generalizing Synthesized Manipulation Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12416)
*Runyi Zhao,Ruixin Wu,Chengkun Li,Hongrui Zhang,Ang Li,Ruixing Jin,Yueci Deng,Yingying Guo,Lihe Ding,Shaocong Dong,Tianfan Xue,Yanjun Gao,Yudong Luo,Pascal Poupart,Simo Wu,Kui Jia,Wei-shi Zheng,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出RoboSynChallenge竞赛，通过生成合成数据与真实世界评估相结合，推动机器人操作策略的泛化性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人操作在泛化能力上存在明显不足，主要由于缺乏多样且大量的真实世界数据，限制了模型和算法的发展。

Method: RoboSynChallenge建立了统一基准，结合大规模合成状态-动作数据生成和标准化的真实机器人评测，参赛者可利用合成试验训练策略，最终在未见过的真实环境中评测泛化能力。配套提供多种基线实现以利对比与复现。

Result: 通过模拟训练与真实验证结合，有望提升数据利用率和模型泛化性。实验涵盖多种任务、环境和难度，实现了综合评估。

Conclusion: RoboSynChallenge为开发通用、数据高效且适应性强的机器人操作系统奠定基础，促进通用型机器人智能的实现。

Abstract: Achieving generalizable robotic manipulation remains a central challenge in embodied intelligence. Despite rapid advances in model architectures and learning algorithms, progress is often limited by the scarcity and narrow diversity of real-world data. The RoboSynChallenge competition introduces a unified benchmark to evaluate and advance the generalizability of manipulation policies across a spectrum of tasks, environments, and difficulty levels. To alleviate the shortage of realistic data, the challenge integrates large-scale synthetic data generation with standardized real-world robotic evaluation. Participants are encouraged to leverage synthesized state-action trials to improve general-purpose policy learning, while final assessments are conducted exclusively on unseen real-world manipulation environments. Baseline implementations, including Transformer-, Diffusion-, Vision-Language-Action, and World-Action-Model-based policies, are provided to ensure reproducibility and comparability. By coupling scalable simulation-based training with rigorous real-world validation, RoboSynChallenge aims to foster the development of broadly capable, data-efficient, and adaptable manipulation systems, thereby paving the way toward truly general robotic intelligence.

</details>


### [165] [Attune: A Self-Annotation Tool for Understanding Robot Operator Attention Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12650)
*Puqi Zhou,Sungsoo Ray Hong,David Porfirio*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了Attune工具，可以帮助多机器人系统开发者更好地理解和设计如何吸引或分散操作员的注意力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人远程监控任务中，一个人通常需同时监督多台机器人，如何有效管理操作员的注意力是界面与机器人行为设计的重大挑战，目前缺乏针对机器人行为对注意力影响的实证指导。

Method: 作者开发了Attune工具，利用操作员视线追踪数据自动检测注意力变化，并通过AI辅助标注注意力变化原因，总结操作员注视模式。随后通过用户实验，让参与者标注视觉触发因素，评估工具有效性。

Result: 实验揭示了操作员注视模式存在较大差异，Attune工具能够有效帮助分析并特征化操作员注意力变化及其诱因。

Conclusion: Attune为界面和行为设计提供了实证参考，有助于机器人开发者调节系统以适应不同类型操作员的注意力特征，提升多机器人系统的监控效率。

Abstract: Deploying robot fleets in complex, real-world environments requires human operators to supervise multiple robots simultaneously. Managing operator attention is a fundamental challenge of designing multi-robot supervision interfaces, encompassing both feed layout and feed content (i.e., robot behavior design). Thus far, designers lack empirical guidance on the latter-how to change a robot's behavior to capture, sustain, or relinquish operator attention during multi-robot supervision. In our vision of the future, designers should be able to use this guidance to calibrate robot behavior to different operator attention profiles. Treating operator eye gaze as a robot behavior design clue, we created a pre-deployment elicitation tool called Attune. Attune automatically identifies when meaningful gaze shifts occur, provides AI assistance for annotating why shifts occurred, and outputs a summary of operator gaze patterns for operator review. We evaluated Attune through a user study in which participants annotated the visual triggers that drew their attention. Our findings unveil variation in observed gaze patterns and reveal how Attune helps characterize operator attention.

</details>


### [166] [FUSE: Active Functional Affordance Grounding through Adaptive Semantic-Geometric Evidence Acquisition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12683)
*Zhou Chen,Sathyanarayanan N. Aakur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种让智能体通过探索环境主动获取证据，以寻找并定位满足特定功能需求的物体的方法，并证明其性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在实际环境中往往需要依据功能而不是身份来识别和交互对象，但当前的功能属性（affordance）定位方法仅依赖固定视角，无法主动获取更多证据应对功能线索遮挡或不完整的问题。

Method: 提出了Active Functional Affordance Grounding新任务，并设计了FUSE框架：融合语义-几何证据的获取，通过不确定性引导的主动探索结合训练得到的高效规划器，有选择地采集信息量大的视角观察。作者还基于Habitat环境构建了基准进行评测。

Result: 实验结果显示，FUSE在非oracle条件下实现了最高的功能定位准确率，并且相比完全显式探索减少了1.33倍的计算，还能适应多种功能属性知识来源。

Conclusion: FUSE能够有效提升功能性物体定位的效率和准确率，推动了主动智能代理在实际复杂环境下的应用。

Abstract: Embodied agents must often identify and interact with objects based on their function rather than their identity, requiring them to actively acquire observations that reveal discriminative functional evidence. Existing affordance grounding methods operate from fixed viewpoints and lack mechanisms for deciding where to look when functional cues are occluded or incomplete. We introduce Active Functional Affordance Grounding, a new task in which an agent sequentially explores a scene to identify and spatially ground an object satisfying a functional query. To address this problem, we propose FUSE, an adaptive semantic-geometric evidence acquisition framework that combines explicit uncertainty-driven exploration with a learned amortized planner to efficiently select informative viewpoints. We further introduce a Habitat-based benchmark for evaluating active functional grounding. Experiments show that FUSE achieves the highest observed non-oracle grounding performance while reducing computation by 1.33x relative to fully explicit exploration, and remains effective across multiple affordance knowledge sources.

</details>


### [167] [SAP-Nav: Spatial Semantic Representation Meets Active Perception for Hierarchical Open-Vocabulary Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12707)
*Xuetong Pei,Jian Liu,Vidura Munasinghe,Bo Miao,U-Xuan Tan,Wenrui Ding,Na Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了SAP-Nav，一个用于分层开放词汇物体导航（OVON）的无训练框架，通过主动感知提升在未知环境下的导航和目标验证能力，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放词汇物体导航任务中，智能体需根据描述性、层次化的自由指令在未知环境中寻找目标。传统方法在仅有局部视野下难以兼顾全局空间理解和准确目标验证，限制性能。

Method: SAP-Nav为一个全在线、零样本（zero-shot）导航系统。它主动采集房间视角，逐步构建可查询的空间-语义表示，使任何已探索位置可用于空间语义检索。此外，引入主动视角验证机制，在验证目标前，智能体会判断并移动到能获取更多信息的位置，提高验证准确率。此方法无需任务特定训练或预先计算场景地图，能兼容分类级及分层导航。

Result: 在LangMap和HM3D-OVON数据集上，SAP-Nav总表现最佳，尤其在region-level导航上相较有训练方法提升了12.2%的SR（成功率）。真实机器人实验也显示出其实用性。

Conclusion: SAP-Nav实现了高效准确的分层与标准物体导航，为无训练、开放环境下的多层级目标查找提供了新思路，有良好实际应用前景。

Abstract: Hierarchical open-vocabulary object navigation (OVON) requires agents to follow free-form instructions that may specify targets through scene-, room-, region-, and instance-level cues in unseen environments. Although recent work LangMap has formalized this setting, reliably solving it under partial observations remains challenging: spatial grounding requires persistent environment-level evidence, whereas target verification requires clear and discriminative candidate views. We present SAP-Nav, a fully online, zero-shot framework that addresses both requirements through active perception. SAP-Nav incrementally constructs a Queryable Spatial-Semantic Representation from actively acquired room views, enabling spatial semantic queries from any explored location. It further employs Active Viewpoint Verification to assess whether the current observation provides sufficient evidence and, when necessary, reposition the agent to a more informative viewpoint before verifying candidates against category and attribute constraints. Although designed for hierarchical OVON, SAP-Nav supports both hierarchical and standard category-level OVON without task-specific training or precomputed scene maps. Experiments on LangMap and HM3D-OVON show that SAP-Nav achieves the overall best performance, including a 12.2% improvement in SR over training-based methods on region-level navigation. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate its practical feasibility. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [168] [Genetic Fuzzy System-Based Multi-Robot Coordination for Planetary Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12755)
*Daegyun Choi,Donghoon Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于遗传模糊系统的分布式多机器人协同搬运任务方法，在复杂地形下优化路径并避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多机器人系统在非结构环境下协同搬运物体时，如何在避障的同时最小化总路径长度的问题。

Method: 首先将地形高程图转化为二维可通行性地图；再利用地形坡度分析区分可通过与不可通过区域。通过遗传算法优化模糊推理系统，使系统在多种复杂场景下调整机器人速度。最后在多种测试环境中验证所提方法。

Result: 所训练的模糊推理系统在转换后的可通行性地图与多种环境场景下测试，展现了良好的路径优化和避障能力。

Conclusion: 该分布式遗传模糊系统能有效提高多机器人协同运输任务在复杂不规则地形中的路径优化和避障表现。

Abstract: This paper proposes a decentralized approach for a multi-robot system (MRS) using a genetic fuzzy system to perform a collaborative object transportation task that minimizes the total path length of the MRS in unstructured environment while avoiding obstacles. For an environment given by an elevation map, terrain traversability analysis with respect to the slope is performed to reduce the dimension and identify non-traversable areas that can be considered as obstacles, and the given map is converted into a traversability map in two dimensional space. In the training process, proposed fuzzy inference systems (FISs) to generate the MRS's velocity for transporting an object to a target position are optimized by a genetic algorithm with several scenarios, such as a local minima, a target that is close to an obstacle, and a cluttered environment. The trained FIS models are applied to the testing environment, which is the converted traversability map, and validated using multiple scenarios.

</details>


### [169] [AirForesight: Current-to-Future Spatial Map Imagination with Cross-Space Planning Consistency for UAV-VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12835)
*Yutong Liu,Xiaojie Li,Mingzhu Xu,Jianlong Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于无人机视觉-语言导航的新框架AirForesight，通过“当前-未来空间图想象”提升导航能力，相较现有方法在公开数据集上表现更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机视觉-语言导航方法大多直接从视觉-语言输入到动作，局部场景理解和空间推理能力有限。作者希望提升无人机对复杂三维环境中指令的理解和空间结构的推断能力，尤其关注未来运动规划的空间推理。

Method: AirForesight框架首先通过多视角观测学习当前场景的结构化空间图，同时监督当前图重建和未来轨迹预测，以编码场景结构和运动意图。利用结构化因果注意力传播当前的空间知识到未来地图推理，将当前与未来表达聚合以预测下一个三维导航点。此外，通过跨空间规划一致损失，鼓励预测轨迹方向与专家动作方向保持一致。

Result: 在OpenUAV和AerialVLN-S数据集上的实验，包括大量消融实验，显示AirForesight在导航任务中取得了强性能，验证了方法的有效性与稳定性。

Conclusion: AirForesight提升了无人机对自然语言导航指令的理解及三维空间推理能力，实现更精确的运动规划，对复杂场景中的视觉-语言导航有显著作用。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle Vision-Language Navigation (UAV-VLN) requires agents to follow language instructions, infer spatial structure from sparse multi-view observations, and execute feasible 3D motion in complex outdoor environments. Despite recent progress with large language models, most existing methods still map vision-language inputs directly to actions, providing limited explicit scene grounding and future-aware spatial reasoning. We propose AirForesight, a current-to-future spatial map imagination framework for UAV-VLN. AirForesight first learns a structured current-map representation from multi-view observations. This representation is jointly supervised by current-map reconstruction and future-trajectory prediction, encouraging it to encode both present scene structure and future motion intent. Under structured causal attention, the current spatial knowledge is propagated to future-map reasoning, and the resulting current and future representations are aggregated to predict the next 3D waypoint. To make spatial imagination more relevant to navigation, we introduce a cross-space planning consistency loss that encourages directional agreement between the predicted map-space trajectory and the expert action direction derived from the ground-truth waypoint displacement. Experiments on OpenUAV and AerialVLN-S, together with extensive ablations, demonstrate strong performance and support the effectiveness and stability of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [170] [ASPIRE-VINS: Adaptive Spline-based Visual-inertial Navigation System With Robust 3D Measurement Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12840)
*Kwangyik Jung,Eungchang Mason Lee,Taekjun Oh,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种名为ASPIRE-VINS的连续时间视觉惯性导航方法，结合了自适应节点分布、多分辨率样条和三维测量空间残差，实现了在不同运动和传感条件下更具竞争力的轨迹估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前离散时间VINS利用IMU预积分，虽准确高效，但基于关键帧的轨迹表示在需要随时评估残差或需运动相关时间分辨率时不够灵活。连续时间样条能平滑表征轨迹，但均匀分布节点可能难以兼顾剧烈运动和静止时区。因而亟需一种自适应且高效的连续时间轨迹建模方案。

Method: 提出ASPIRE-VINS框架，融合三大创新：（1）自适应节点分配（AKP）根据局部运动变化自动调整节点稀疏度；（2）多分辨率样条（MRS）在切空间实现受限区域的局部轨迹优化；（3）三维测量空间残差（3D-MSR）在3D测量空间对视觉特征和实际观测射线对齐以增强一致性。

Result: 在多种运动和传感条件下，与现有基线方法对比，ASPIRE-VINS展现出更优或相当的轨迹误差，尤其在动态变化剧烈或静止区间均有良好表现。

Conclusion: ASPIRE-VINS有效提升了VINS在不同运动状态下的轨迹建模能力，证明了运动自适应连续时间模型的有效性和广泛适用性。

Abstract: Visual-inertial navigation systems estimate six-degree-of-freedom motion by fusing visual and inertial data. Modern discrete-time methods with IMU preintegration provide strong accuracy and efficiency, but keyframe-based representations can be less flexible when residuals must be evaluated at arbitrary timestamps or when motion-dependent temporal resolution is needed. Continuous-time splines address this issue by representing the trajectory as a smooth temporal function, but uniformly spaced knots can under-represent rapid dynamics or over-parameterize static intervals. This letter proposes ASPIRE-VINS, a continuous-time VINS framework that combines adaptive knot placement (AKP), multi-resolution splines (MRS), and 3D measurement-space residuals (3D-MSR). AKP allocates knots according to local motion variation, MRS adds bounded local refinement in tangent space, and 3D-MSR provides bearing consistency by aligning transformed features with calibrated observation rays in 3D measurement space. Experiments show that ASPIRE-VINS achieves competitive or lower trajectory errors than the compared baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of motion-adaptive continuous-time trajectory modeling under diverse motion and sensing conditions.

</details>


### [171] [BrainWAM: Action-Space Coordination of Semantic Priors and Predictive Dynamics for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12854)
*Bing Zhan,Shuyao Shang,Jiahao Gu,Shuo Lu,Yuan Xu,Zhao Wang,Yida Wang,Xueyang Zhang,Kun Zhan,Lue Fan,Zhaoxiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合语义推理和预测动态的新规划框架 BrainWAM，在自动驾驶任务中实现了有组织的动作空间协同，并显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶方法通常只重视语义约束（如VLA）或预测动态（如WAM）中的一方，无法同时兼顾，导致规划性能受限。因此，亟需一种能同时利用语义和预测动态优势的统一规划模型。

Method: BrainWAM通过将语义推理和世界预测建模转化为两个各自专门的动作导向路径（Action Pathways），并在紧凑的动作表征层面进行对齐，实现功能协作。此外，提出了异步校正流推理策略，采用解耦的视频与动作去噪，从而提升推理效率并保留与规划相关的预测信息。

Result: BrainWAM在NAVSIM v1和v2评测上分别取得89.5 PDMS和89.6 EPDMS的新纪录，性能持续领先于仅采用VLA或WAM的方法。

Conclusion: BrainWAM有效整合了语义和动态信息，为自动驾驶的端到端规划提供了新思路，并且在相关基准测试中显示出显著的实用性和优越性。

Abstract: Autonomous driving requires planning under both semantic constraints and predictive dynamics. Existing end-to-end driving approaches, however, typically emphasize only one side of this requirement: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exploit VLM priors for semantic reasoning, while World Action Models (WAMs) provide future-aware prediction through generative world modeling. This naturally motivates a unified planner that can leverage both semantic priors and predictive dynamics. However, we find that a naive combination through joint token-level attention suffers from an attention-allocation mismatch, where semantic shortcuts dominate the shared attention space and suppress predictive dynamics. Inspired by neuroscience evidence that complex behavior arises from coordination among functionally specialized systems, we propose BrainWAM, a structured action-space coordination framework that converts semantic reasoning and predictive world modeling into two specialized action-oriented pathways, and aligns them at the level of compact action representations. We further introduce an asynchronous rectified-flow inference strategy with decoupled video and action denoising, which shortens inference latency while preserving planning-relevant predictive context. BrainWAM reaches state-of-the-art performance on both NAVSIM v1 (89.5 PDMS) and NAVSIM v2 (89.6 EPDMS), consistently outperforming VLA-only or WAM-only methods, highlighting BrainWAM as a practical and promising direction for autonomous driving systems.

</details>


### [172] [HumanoidVLN: A Physics-Grounded Simulator and Benchmark for Vision-Language Navigation Across Diverse Humanoid Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12860)
*Quan-Dung Pham,Anh Dao,The-Anh Nguyen,Minh Nguyen-Dinh,Phuong Nam Dang,Tri Pham,Hung Tran,Bach Dao,Tuyen P. Le,Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 作者提出了HumanoidVLN，这是一个针对双足机器人视觉-语言导航（VLN）的物理仿真平台及基准，支持多种机器人和VLN模型，并提供丰富、经过校验的任务数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前VLN基准主要针对轮式机器人，未考虑双足机器人在物理、运动学等方面的特殊挑战，比如身体约束、不同形态导致的差异，以及由步行产生的摄像头动态畸变。因此，需要一个专门面向类人机器人的VLN仿真基准。

Method: 搭建了一个基于NVIDIA Isaac Sim的仿真平台，支持多种双足机器人，采用分层控制策略（强化学习行走+可替换的PD或MPC路径跟踪器），并通过艺术家设计和3D重建丰富环境。任务指令由多智能体生成、人工审核，确保多样化和高质量。支持多种VLN模型，易于扩展新机器人和模型。

Result: 平台涵盖四种机器人和四种VLN模型，任务数据集包括933个带精细和风格化指令的参考回合。在虚拟平台上的实验中，JanusVLN模型表现最佳（成功率43.55%、nDTW 48.38）。初步仿真到实物实验显示，导航误差和实际执行高度相关，轨迹相似度高。

Conclusion: HumanoidVLN扩充了VLN领域的物理真实性和平台多样性，体现了模型、控制器和机器人物理属性的复杂交互，为未来类人机器人在现实环境中的导航研究提供了重要工具和基准。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) for humanoid robots poses challenges existing benchmarks fail to address: bipedal locomotion imposes physical constraints absent from wheeled agents, humanoid morphologies vary across platforms, and egocentric observations are distorted by locomotion-induced camera dynamics. We present HumanoidVLN, a physics-grounded simulator and benchmark for VLN across diverse humanoid embodiments. Built on NVIDIA Isaac Sim, our platform supports an extensible set of humanoid configurations, demonstrated on four robots (Unitree G1, Unitree H1, Internal-A, Internal-B) spanning 10-12 lower-body DoF and heights from 1.17m to 1.80m, via a hierarchical control stack combining a reinforcement learning locomotion policy with interchangeable PD or MPC path trackers. New robots and VLN models integrate with minimal effort; we demonstrate compatibility with NaVILA, DualVLN, StreamVLN, and JanusVLN. Environments are drawn from artist-designed scenes and 3D Gaussian Splatting reconstructions, filtered for navigable areas exceeding 100 square meters. Instructions are generated by a dual generator-reviewer plus paraphraser multi-agent pipeline with human-in-the-loop verification, yielding 933 collision-aware reference episodes, each paired with one fine-grained instruction and three coarse-grained stylistic variants (formal, natural, casual). Across four models and four embodiments, JanusVLN achieves the highest mean success rate of 43.55% and nDTW of 48.38. In a 20-episode sim-to-real pilot with DualVLN and the Unitree G1, navigation errors correlate strongly (r=0.935), with a mean absolute difference of 0.68m and mean trajectory similarity of 0.782 (+/-0.188) nDTW. These results highlight the interaction between VLN models, controllers, and humanoid embodiments under physical execution. Code, benchmark, and data will be released upon acceptance at https://humanoid-vln.github.io/.

</details>


### [173] [AMR-Pose: An Active LED Marker-Based Relative Pose Estimation Framework With Probabilistic Switching PnP for Cooperative AUVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12866)
*Zeyu Sha,Xiaorui Wang,Mingyang Yang,Feitian Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种基于LED主动标记的新型水下机器人相对位姿估计算法AMR-Pose，并通过实验验证了其在恶劣水下环境中的鲁棒性和实时性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下自主机器人间的精准协作严重依赖于可靠的相对位姿估计。然而，水下视觉环境复杂，常存在浑浊、光照变化、反射、遮挡等问题，导致传统视觉方法效果不佳。该论文旨在解决这些环境恶劣条件下AUV间相对定位的困难。

Method: 提出AMR-Pose框架，在领航AUV上集成红色中心LED与蓝色外周LED组成的新型主动标记模块；并设计了PSwPnP（概率切换的n点位姿估计）算法，结合SE(3)李群姿态传播、概率化标记关联和可见性自适应测量融合，实现了动态响应标记可见性的鲁棒六自由度相对位姿估计。

Result: 在水箱实验（含高精度运动捕捉系统地面实测）下，AMR-Pose能在不同可见性、部分遮挡等复杂水下条件实现准确、平滑、鲁棒的相对位姿估计。闭环跟随实验亦展示了其实时应用能力。

Conclusion: AMR-Pose在复杂水下环境下为AUV提供了鲁棒、实时的相对位姿估计，解决了经典视觉方法易受环境干扰的问题，为多AUV协作提供了有力支撑。

Abstract: Reliable relative pose estimation between autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) is critical for cooperative ocean exploration, sampling, and multi-robot coordination. However, achieving robust vision-based relative localization in underwater environments remains challenging due to severe optical degradation, including turbidity, illumination variations, reflections, and intermittent feature occlusions. This paper presents AMR-Pose, an active LED marker-based relative pose estimation framework for cooperative AUVs. A compact marker module consisting of one red central LED and three blue peripheral LEDs is developed and integrated onto the leader AUV to provide distinctive visual features under complex underwater conditions. Building upon the detected marker observations, a probabilistic switching Perspective-n-Point estimator (PSwPnP) is developed by combining Lie-group pose propagation on $SE(3)$, probabilistic marker association, and visibility-adaptive measurement fusion for robust six-degree-of-freedom relative pose estimation. The proposed framework dynamically adapts the estimation process according to marker visibility, maintaining geometric consistency and temporal stability during partial observations and visibility transitions. Extensive water-tank experiments with motion-capture ground truth validate that AMR-Pose achieves accurate, smooth, and robust relative pose estimation under challenging underwater conditions. Closed-loop leader-follower experiments further demonstrate its feasibility for real-time relative pose feedback in cooperative underwater robotics.

</details>


### [174] [Temporal GRPO: Beyond Trajectory-Level Credit in Vision-Language-Action Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13026)
*Yao Zhou,Hang Gao,Fengge Wu,Changwen Zheng,Wenwen Qiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Temporal GRPO的新方法，通过引入任务阶段，提升了基于结果反馈的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)策略后训练的信效分配与样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统GRPO（Generalized Rollout Policy Optimization）方法在VLA后训练中，将同一条轨迹的优势均匀分布于所有动作，但这样会导致部分由早期正确动作取得的进展在后期失败后被不公正地惩罚，即‘轨迹级信效别名’问题。

Method: Temporal GRPO方法通过将任务分解为可检测的阶段，对每一个阶段划分特定的动作区间，比对进入同一阶段的轨迹，仅在本阶段计算优势，并将其应用于对应的动作区间，从而实现更细粒度的信效分配。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0平台上，该方法提升了任务成功率和样本利用效率，并在不同任务长度上表现一致。在LIBERO-Long上，分阶段更新可维护共享的前置阶段，并能在轨迹结果首次分化的关键阶段集中提升。

Conclusion: Temporal GRPO通过细粒度的阶段信效分配，改善了VLA任务的后期训练效果，无论在成功率还是样本效率上均优于传统方法，并有效解决了轨迹级信效分配不公的问题。

Abstract: Outcome-driven reinforcement learning offers a scalable way to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) policies from sparse task-success feedback. In common GRPO-based VLA post-training, one rollout-level advantage is applied to every action in the trajectory. A rollout that completes several valid stages but fails later can therefore penalize the actions that produced its earlier progress. We call this trajectory-level credit aliasing. Temporal GRPO addresses this problem by constructing detectable task stages, aligning each rollout with stage-specific action intervals, and comparing only rollouts that have entered the same stage. The resulting stage advantages are applied to their corresponding intervals in a single policy update. On RoboTwin 2.0, Temporal GRPO improves task success and sample efficiency, with consistent gains across task horizons. Controlled updates on LIBERO-Long preserve shared prerequisite stages and concentrate improvement at the first stage where rollout outcomes diverge.

</details>


### [175] [H2R-Bench: Benchmarking Human-to-Robot Manipulation Video Generation in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13049)
*Dingyi Rong,Yue Shi,Chaofan Ma,Jiezhang Cao,Zongrui Wang,Zeyu Zhang,Yao Mu,Guangtao Zhai,Ning Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了H2R-Bench，一个用于评估跨形态人类到机器人操作视频生成的基准，填补了人类操控视频如何转化为机器人操作视频的研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习需要大量操作数据，而收集真实机器人演示昂贵且难以扩展。虽然人类头戴视角操作视频丰富，但由于人体和机器人的外形差异，这些数据难以直接迁移到机器人上。因此亟需一种方法桥接此差异，高效利用人类演示促进机器人学习。

Method: 作者构建了H2R-Bench基准，要求模型将人类第一视角操作视频转化为特定机器人形态下的操作视频。基准中每个任务都包括：人类演示视频、目标机器人形态约束、任务目标、动作事件、功能性接触及物体反应等标注。评估指标包括目标完成、动作完成、接触转移、形态正确性及视频质量。作者在6类操作任务、2种机器人形态上，对11个主流视频生成模型进行了测试。

Result: 实验结果显示，当前视频生成模型在人类到机器人操控的迁移上表现有限，即使最先进的模型也常常在机器人形态一致性、功能交互和任务执行上出现失败。

Conclusion: H2R-Bench为检验和诊断视频模型能否跨越人-机器人形态鸿沟，并有效将人类操作视频转化为机器人训练数据提供了系统化评测框架，有望推动跨形态操作视频生成领域发展。

Abstract: Large-scale manipulation data is essential for robot learning, yet collecting robot demonstrations remains expensive and difficult to scale. Meanwhile, abundant egocentric human manipulation videos provide rich behavioral experiences, but transferring them across embodiments remains challenging due to differences between human hands and robotic end-effectors. Recent advances in video world models offer a promising pathway to synthesize robot-centric manipulation videos from human observations, while their cross-embodiment transfer capability remains largely unexplored. Therefore, we introduce H2R-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating cross-embodiment human-to-robot manipulation video generation, where models transform egocentric human demonstrations into robot manipulation videos under specified embodiments. Each benchmark instance contains a human demonstration video, target embodiment constraints, and source-grounded annotations covering task goals, action events, functional contacts, and object responses. H2R-Bench evaluates generated videos through five dimensions, including goal-state completion, action-event completion, functional contact transfer, embodiment correctness, and general video quality. We benchmark eleven state-of-the-art video generation models across six manipulation families and two robot embodiments. Our evaluation reveals that current video world models remain limited in human-to-robot manipulation transfer: even leading models often fail in embodiment consistency, functional interaction, and task execution. H2R-Bench provides a systematic diagnostic framework for evaluating whether video world models can bridge the human-to-robot embodiment gap and convert human manipulation observations into robot-centric training resources.

</details>


### [176] [Semantic Radiance Fields as Simulators for Spatial Reasoning in Real-World Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13095)
*Nico Heider,Michał Jan Włodarczyk,Katarzyna Wasielewska-Michniewska,Przemysław Hołda,Martin Schieck,Marcin Paprzycki,Maria Ganzha,Bogdan Franczyk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了利用语义辐射场（SRF）作为模拟器，为具身智能体的空间推理训练与评估提供兼顾几何真实性和语义可查询性的环境解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成模拟器虽然能提供语义真值，但缺乏真实性；基于真实环境重建的模拟器虽然真实，却默认不具备语义标签，难以兼得。为此亟需一种既真实又可语义查询的环境表示。

Method: 提出使用语义辐射场（SRF）：将2D语义分割结果提升到3D辐射场，联合编码几何、外观和逐类语义信息。SRF通过对真实场景进行RGB图像采集与三维重建，能支持新视角渲染、语义及自由空间查询。

Result: SRF能高效生成多样的真实世界环境，用于空间推理模型的训练和评测。以果园采苹果任务为例，SRF为物理引擎提供相机渲染、语义真值和占用信息。

Conclusion: SRF实现了兼具真实性和可语义查询的模拟环境，促进了具身智能体空间推理研究的环境基础升级。

Abstract: Training and evaluating spatial reasoning in embodied agents requires diverse environments that are both geometrically faithful and semantically queryable. Synthetic simulators offer ground truth semantics but sacrifice realism; simulators based on reconstructions of real-world environments have realistic appearance but lack ground truth semantics by default. We propose using Semantic Radiance Fields (SRF) as simulators for spatial reasoning agents. SRFs are a representation that unifies these requirements by lifting 2D semantic segmentations from pretrained vision models into a 3D radiance field that jointly encodes geometry, appearance, and per-class semantic identity. The resulting fields are reconstructed from posed RGB captures of real scenes and support novel-view synthesis, semantic and free-space queries within a single grounded representation. This enables the efficient generation of diverse real-world environments to train and evaluate spatial reasoning models. As an example application, we outline an SRF-driven simulator for an orchard apple-reaching task, in which the radiance field supplies camera rendering, semantic ground truth, and occupancy queries to a physics engine.

</details>


### [177] [S2-HWM: Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model for Long-Horizon Surgical Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13103)
*Shuzhe Zhang,Xin Zhu,Yinling Qian,Qiong Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对外科机器人长时序操作任务提出了一种稀疏事件分层世界模型S2-HWM，在PegTransfer任务上相比基线算法大幅提升了成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时序的手术机器人操控任务面临稀疏奖励和不规则关键事件，现有方法难以显式刻画任务进度和阶段划分，手动分段难以准确反映任务状态变化。

Method: 提出Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model (S2-HWM)，学习从原始潜在轨迹中自动提取关键事件证据，并用事件级管理器协调目标，按需更新primitive worker的潜在goal。基于这些事件，构建事件转移模型（ETM）预测分段边界、持续时间和累计奖励，实现更长时延推理以辅助经理学习；底层worker仍用常规actor-critic方法。

Result: 在基于SurRoL的PegTransfer任务上，S2-HWM成功率达98.7%，较无分层事件结构的DreamerV3基线高22.7个百分点。

Conclusion: S2-HWM通过自适应的稀疏事件分层结构，有效提升了机器人在长时序稀疏奖励任务中的表现，优于传统平坦世界模型方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon surgical robot manipulation is challenging because task rewards are sparse, while meaningful interaction changes occur at irregular intervals. Existing world-model agents typically imagine at primitive-step resolution, leaving variable-duration task progress implicit. Manually specified stages can provide intermediate structure, but their task specific boundaries are difficult to align with state-dependent interaction transitions. We propose S2-HWM, a Sparse Event-Structured Hierarchical World Model that learns sparse event evidence from primitive latent trajectories to coordinate an event-level manager and a primitive-step worker. The event evidence schedules manager goal updates, and each selected latent goal conditions the worker's primitive actions until the next update. The learned event evidence also forms variable-duration segments for an Event Transition Model (ETM), which predicts the next?boundary stochastic state, segment duration, and accumulated segment reward. Chaining these event-level predictions provides a variable-duration continuation beyond the primitive imagination horizon for manager learning, while the worker retains primitive-step actor-critic learning. On a SurRoL-based PegTransfer task, S2-HWM achieves a success rate of 98.7%, outperforming the flat GAS DreamerV3 baseline by 22.7 percentage points.

</details>


### [178] [FAM-DQ: A Dual-Quadrotor-Based Fully Actuated Aerial Manipulator for High-Torque Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13220)
*Xuwei Yang,Ruoyu Ren,Ziqian Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型双四旋翼全驱动空中操作机械臂（FAM-DQ），能够实现高扭矩物理交互，并实现了6自由度末端执行器的精准控制，实验验证了其高扭矩输出和良好的操控性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统欠驱动空中机械臂存在姿态与位置强耦合，难以实现精准操作；而全驱动设计虽然可解耦但结构复杂、有效载荷小且扭矩输出不足。因此需要设计兼具高输出与灵活性的空中操作平台，以满足高扭矩物理交互等复杂任务需求。

Method: 提出FAM-DQ结构：在中央框架两端各安装一个四旋翼推进模块，并通过无动力关节连接，用齿轮驱动的舵机调节方向，实现全向控制。通过这种新颖构型，实现了末端6自由度解耦控制和增强的扭矩输出。

Result: 通过轨迹跟踪、姿态跟踪、静态扭矩测量和旋紧螺丝等实验，验证了FAM-DQ的设计与性能。该系统最大输出扭矩为1.019 N·m，总质量0.447 kg，扭矩质量比达到2.28 N·m/kg，性能优于传统设计。

Conclusion: FAM-DQ标志着高效全驱动空中操作机械臂的新突破，在结构简化的同时显著提升了操作扭矩和灵活性，为复杂空中物理交互任务提供了可靠平台。

Abstract: Aerial physical interaction requires aerial manipulation platforms to generate large interaction forces and torques while maintaining precise end-effector control. However, conventional underactuated aerial manipulators suffer from strong position-attitude coupling, whereas fully actuated platform designs often face structural complexity, limited payload capacity, and insufficient torque output. This paper presents FAM-DQ, a dual-quadrotor based fully actuated aerial manipulator designed for high-torque physical interaction tasks. By mounting two quadrotor propulsion modules at the ends of a central frame through passive joints, while using a gear-driven servo to regulate the pointing direction, FAM-DQ achieves decoupled $6$-DoF end-effector control with omnidirectional manipulation capability and enhanced torque output. Experiments including trajectory tracking, attitude tracking, static torque measurement, and screw driving validate the proposed design. FAM-DQ achieves a maximum torque of $1.019~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m}$ with a total mass of $0.447~\mathrm{kg}$, corresponding to a torque-to-mass ratio of $2.28~\mathrm{N}\cdot\mathrm{m/kg}$.

</details>


### [179] [Manufacturing Complex Airtight Soft Pneumatic Actuators for Soft Robotics: Process Evaluation and Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13233)
*Mohammed Abboodi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对用于制造复杂软气动执行器的多种制造方法进行了评估，发现熔融沉积建模（FDM）在工艺优化后最为灵活，文中总结了各工艺的优缺点并提出了面向制造的设计指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制造复杂软气动执行器时需同时兼顾几何精度、柔顺性、结构完整性和气密性，而目前实现这些要求具有挑战性，因此需系统评估和改进不同制造工艺。

Method: 作者对热收缩成型、硅胶铸造、基于粉末和液体的增材制造、熔融沉积建模（FDM）等多种制造路径进行了工艺筛选、基线制造、失效分析与工艺改进，比较各方法的工艺局限性与可优化问题。

Result: 热收缩成型受限于几何一致性，硅胶铸造受制于模具可达性，粉末/液体增材制造方法存在残余材料堵塞，光固化方法依赖后处理与材料性能。FDM可以通过工艺优化逐步减少缺陷；气密性受壁厚和挤出路径影响。

Conclusion: 研究提出了面向制造的设计方法，建议根据执行器结构与制造工艺约束进行匹配，为软机器人应用中复杂、柔韧且气密的软气动执行器开发提供了实用性指导。

Abstract: Manufacturing complex soft pneumatic actuators remains challenging because geometric fidelity, compliance, structural integrity, and airtightness must be achieved simultaneously. This study presents a manufacturing-focused evaluation of several fabrication routes for complex pneumatic structures, including heat-shrink forming, silicone casting, powder- and liquid-based additive manufacturing, and fused deposition modeling (FDM). The processes were assessed through process screening, baseline fabrication, failure analysis, and process improvement to distinguish inherent process limitations from correctable manufacturing defects. Heat-shrink forming was limited by geometric conformity, casting by mold accessibility and bonded interfaces, powder-based methods by residual material trapped within enclosed passages, and digital light processing by the material properties and post-processing requirements of the investigated system. FDM provided the most adaptable route because its dominant defects could be progressively reduced through process optimization. The results further showed that airtightness depends not only on nominal wall thickness but also on extrusion-path architecture, while support-free geometry is important when access for internal post-processing is limited. These findings establish a practical design-for-manufacturing approach in which process selection is guided by the compatibility between actuator architecture and manufacturing constraints. The proposed approach provides practical guidance for developing complex, flexible, and airtight soft pneumatic actuators for soft robotic applications

</details>


### [180] [Predictive Relative-Velocity Steering for Safe Robotic Manipulator Teleoperation in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13284)
*Changhao Hu,Zeyi Liu,Songqiao Hu,Shuang Liu,Zihan Meng,Xiao He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级、模块化的主动避碰框架，能实时预测并避免遥操作机械臂端执行器的碰撞，兼顾安全性与高效性，在仿真和实际测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作机械臂在人类操控下能够实现灵巧运动，但在网络延迟或操作员注意力有限的情况下，可能无法及时发现和避免突然出现的障碍物，带来安全隐患。为了解决这一问题，需要一种能够主动预防碰撞且适合远程高频控制的避障方法。

Method: 该框架基于端执行器速度指令，先对点云信息预处理，通过集成超调保护的TTC（碰撞时间）预测潜在碰撞，然后利用Rodrigues旋转公式修正相对速度方向（保持其幅值不变），有效避免传统人工势场方法中易出现的死锁问题。此外，预测模块补偿了点云处理的延迟，整个方案设计轻量，满足遥操作高频控制需求。

Result: 在多种仿真场景下，该方法实现了比基线算法更高的端执行器避碰率。并在物理机器人系统实验中，进一步验证了其避碰效果。

Conclusion: 所提轻量级主动避碰框架能在遥操作下可靠且高效地避免机械臂碰撞，提升了安全性，兼具理论创新和实际应用价值。

Abstract: Recent advances in teleoperation have enabled robotic manipulators to perform dexterous, human-arm-like motions. However, human operators may fail to avoid suddenly appearing obstacles promptly and effectively, particularly under network latency or limited attention, thereby creating safety risks. To address this issue, we propose a lightweight and modular framework for proactive collision avoidance, operating directly at the end-effector velocity-command level. After preprocessing the point cloud, the framework first predicts potential collisions based on time-to-collision (TTC) with integrated overshoot protection, and subsequently rotates the relative-velocity vector using Rodrigues' rotation formula. The deflection changes only the direction of the relative velocity while preserving its magnitude, thereby mitigating the deadlock problem commonly encountered by conventional artificial potential field (APF) methods. The prediction module compensates for point-cloud processing latency introduced by complex teleoperation pipelines, while the lightweight design enables the high-frequency control required for teleoperation. Simulations across diverse scenarios show that the proposed method achieves a higher end-effector collision avoidance rate than the baseline methods. Experiments on a physical robotic system further validate its collision-avoidance effectiveness.

</details>


### [181] [NestDex: Nested Policy Learning with Copilot Assisted Teleoperation for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13362)
*James Zhao,Jinhe Tang,Mingyuan Ba,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为NestDex的嵌套策略学习框架，通过利用学习得到的手部技能，简化灵巧操作机器人的演示收集过程。该方法提升了演示的可靠性和效率，同时支持高效的自主策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在灵巧操作任务中，操作员需要精确协调手指和手臂的运动，使得演示数据的收集变得困难且效率低下。现有方法往往需要高质量、全任务的演示，制约了灵巧操作行为的学习。

Method: NestDex采用了分层嵌套策略架构：①内层手部策略基于本体感觉自适应调整手部动作；②外层由操作员通过单自由度离合控制手臂并激活合适的已学手部技能；③视觉-语言选择器在任务不同阶段激活相应手部技能；④使用手部动作变分自编码器（VAE）对手部动作进行压缩；⑤最终用演示数据训练一套可独立控制手臂和手部的视觉-运动策略。

Result: 实验证明NestDex能在实际灵巧操作场景中提高演示数据的可靠性和收集效率，同时支持更高效的自主策略学习。

Conclusion: NestDex系统有效降低了灵巧操作演示收集门槛和难度，提升了机器人学习复杂操作任务的能力，验证了方法的实用性和有效性。

Abstract: Dexterous manipulation promises substantially richer robot interaction with the physical world, but learning these behaviours remains constrained by the difficulty of collecting consistent, complete-task demonstrations. Unlike parallel-jaw manipulation, dexterous tasks require the operator to coordinate arm motion with precise, contact-rich finger behaviour throughout the task. We introduce NestDex, a nested policy-learning framework that reduces this burden by using learned hand skills to assist demonstration collection. The operator controls the arm and regulates the active hand skill through a single-DoF clutch, rather than directly specifying the full finger trajectory. The inner hand policy adapts its motion from the latest proprioceptive history, while a vision-language selector activates the appropriate skill for each task stage. The resulting demonstrations train a separate outer visuomotor policy that controls both the arm and hand without the inner policies at deployment. A hand-action variational autoencoder provides compact hand-action targets while retaining arm commands in joint space. Across real-world dexterous manipulation experiments, NestDex improves demonstration reliability and efficiency, and the resulting empirical evaluations support effective autonomous policy learning. Video Demo are available at project website https://aus.bot/research/nestdex.

</details>


### [182] [FIRE-VLA: Failure-Informed Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13395)
*Hao Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了FIRE-VLA方法，通过自蒸馏和相对策略优化机制提升自动驾驶中的视觉-语言-行动模型表现，从而更有效地处理失败案例并减少部分重大错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的相对策略优化（如GRPO）只能在失败区域内部进行排序，难以指导模型跳出失败，特别是在采样得到的轨迹质量普遍较差时，改进现有基于强化学习的方法以更好地利用失败经验，实现模型自我进化成为必要。

Method: 提出FIRE-VLA框架，将低奖励、低多样性的轨迹组作为触发信号，进行自蒸馏，即冻结一份原始模型作为教师，学生模型则根据自身生成的前缀接受教师基于未来轨迹的监督。教师和学生参数规模一致，由策略演化实现分布自适应，无需更大外部教师。GRPO机制仍同步应用于所有组。

Result: 在150个nuScenes保留场景中的6,019个样本评测中，FIRE-VLA在单样本规划表现持平的基础上，将G=4平均L2误差从1.848米降至1.5米，持续性失败率从13.03%降至11.2%，误差下降主要来自于极端错误案例的减少。

Conclusion: FIRE-VLA能更有效利用失败信息，实现策略的自适应和自演化，主要缓解了严重失败场景，提升了视觉-语言-行动模型在自动驾驶任务上的整体表现。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning improves autonomous-driving vision-language-action (VLA) models by evaluating trajectories sampled from the current policy. Group relative policy optimization (GRPO) learns from reward differences within each rollout group.
